Structural cue: the verb `يَسْرِ` puts the night itself into motion after the counted nights. Assigned role: night becomes the moving medium through which the hidden sequence advances. Abductive move: I infer that night-travel, often an actor’s movement, is here projected onto the time itself. Reading change: the ayah reads as a passage of concealed process, not a still backdrop.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4ea88d9304baf7ca92f0","text":"2. **Night as the agent of its own uncovering.** **Evidence/cue:** `ل ي ل B001` supplies darkness, while `س ر ي B004` supplies uncovering, removal, and relief; 89:1 places dawn beside the sequence. **Mechanism:** darkness is the cover and the verb's dormant branch performs removal. The packet supplies succession, while I assign the directional arrow from traveling night to cleared concealment. **Reading change:** *yasri* can describe a progressive stripping-away of night, making dawn the achieved relief rather than an unrelated next state.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000702/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5310ef243898e15af1c6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:4:3\",\"root_000702\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gecenin geçişi, karanlığın örtüsünü adım adım kaldıran ve ferahlığa yer açan bir çözülme işlevi görür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000702/B002","root_000702/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5c33fcb33cd7ea75d189","text":"89:9 unexpectedly literalized the flow latent in `س ر ي B002` by supplying the valley as flood channel (`و د ي B005`). This made the earlier uncovering reading secondary but compatible: flow describes the course, while `س ر ي B004` describes what that course accomplishes.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000702/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cdfc697b669a8b26bd41","text":"Later 89:27’s settled soul made `س ر ي B004` relief more visible: the moving-away of night prefigures the removal of inward agitation, not only the arrival of morning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000702/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ebc28c5c86691ecb2c5b","text":"1. **Night moving as a channelized flow.** **Evidence/cue:** `س ر ي B002` supplies a running stream or creeping flow; dawn in 89:1 splits and releases flow (`ف ج ر B001`), and 89:9 names a valley as flood channel (`و د ي B005`). **Mechanism:** the flow branch makes night the moving content of a temporal channel, with dawn its outlet. The packet supplies the two hydraulic endpoints; the mapping from watercourse to temporal passage is inferred. **Reading change:** night does not simply depart but runs through a bounded course toward opening.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000702/B004","root_001132/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f599b6501ddc75b661a4","text":"2. **The passing night as relief/uncovering.** Lexical evidence: `س ر ي B004` uncovering and relief; `ف ج ر B002` dawn breaking from night. Structural cue: 89:1 and 89:4 form a temporal loop: dawn splits; night travels away. Assigned role: `يَسْرِ` removes the covering that made disorder latent. Abductive move: I infer that motion of night causes exposure; the packet supplies dawn and night-travel, while the causality is my construction.
